## Activity

    $ [xml]$d=Get-Content ../Flowchart/Actual-FlowChart.drawio -Raw; $p=$d.mxfile.diagram[0]; $p.mxGraphModel.root.mxCell | Where-Object { $_.id -like 'zot1JTxo4ji1ywPOPgd8-*' -and ([int]($_.id -replace '^.*-','')) -le 74 } | ForEach-Object { $_.OuterXml } | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 review/original-entry-cells.xml; Get-Content Construct.txt -Encoding UTF8 | Select-Object -First 310; Get-Content review/build-flowchart.cjs -First 24
    Initial Functional Assessment of IOTS.
    
    CIRIL IOTS - Functional Structure:
    IOTS
    │
    ├── Authentication & Authorization Layer
    │   │
    │   ├── User Login
    │   ├── Credential Validation
    │   │   ├── Valid → Continue
    │   │   └── Invalid → Notify Invalid Credentials → Return
    │   │
    │   ├── Location Validation
    │   │   ├── Known / Valid → Continue
    │   │   └── Unknown → Mark Session as Remote / Off-site
    │   │
    │   ├── Record Login & Location Coordinates
    │   └── Decide User Role
    │
    └── Management Layer
    |    │
    |    ├── ADMIN
    |    ├── MANAGER
    |    └── EMPLOYEE
    |
    └── Intelligence & Analytical
            │
            ├── Daily Reports
            ├── Weekly Reports
            ├── Monthly Reports
            ├── Role-Based Reports
            ├── Performance Analytics
            ├── Workload Analytics
            ├── Attendance Analytics
            ├── Team Analytics where permitted
            └── Exception / Risk Analysis
    
    
    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    MANAGEMENT LAYER
    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        ADMIN Dashboard:
    
                1. User Management
                    │
                    ├── Create New User
                    ├── Set User Role & Permissions
                    ├── Activate / Deactivate User Account
                    ├── Reset User Password
                    └── Record Changes in Audit Log
    
                2. Location & Attendance Control
                    │
                    ├── Set Office Location Coordinates
                    ├── Define Standard Working Hours
                    ├── Monitor Daily Attendance Records
                    ├── Review Off-site / Flagged Logins
                    └── Update / Record Attendance Result
    
                3. Global Task Management
                    │
                    ├── Monitor All Tasks
                    ├── Select Specific Task for Intervention
                    ├── Edit Task Details
                    ├── Reassign Task
                    ├── Cancel Task
                    ├── Force / Override Approval Where Authorized
                    └── Record Changes in Audit Log
    
                4. Global Announcements
                    │
                    ├── Create New Notice / Message
                    ├── Select Target Audience
                    │   ├── Specific User
                    │   ├── Role-based Audience
                    │   └── All Users
                    ├── Set Message Display Duration
                    ├── Publish to Dashboards
                    └── Record Action
    
                5. System Settings
                    │
                    ├── Access System Registry
                    ├── Create / Manage Roles & Designations
                    ├── Manage Automated Report Settings
                    ├── Update System Notification Templates
                    └── Save Configuration Changes
    
                6. Security & Audit Viewer
                    │
                    ├── Access Security Logs
                    ├── Filter Logs
                    │   ├── By Date
                    │   ├── By User
                    │   └── By Action / Task Status
                    ├── Review User Activity
                    ├── Review Login History
                    ├── Review Task Modification History
                    └── Export Security Reports
    
        MANAGER Dashboard:
                1. Task Management & Workflow
                    │
                    ├── Create a Task
                    ├── Define Task / Deliverables
                    ├── Set Priority & Due Date
                    ├── Check Employee Workload
                    ├── Assign Task to Employee
                    ├── Notify Employee
                    ├── Monitor Task Status
                    ├── Monitor Execution
                    ├── Handle Overdue Tasks
                    ├── Handle Reassignment
                    ├── Receive Submitted Work
                    ├── Review Task
                    ├── Approve Work
                    ├── Provide Feedback / Corrections
                    ├── Request Revision
                    ├── Review Resubmission
                    └── Complete & Close Task
    
                2. Team Management
                    │
                    ├── View Team Members
                    ├── View Employee Profiles
                    ├── View Current Assignments
                    ├── View Employee Workload
                    ├── View Employee Availability
                    └── Track Employee Work Progress
    
                3. Performance Monitoring
                    │
                    ├── Monitor Task Completion Rate
                    ├── Review Overdue Tasks
                    ├── Track Deadlines
                    ├── Identify Performance Issues
                    └── Give Feedback
    
                4. Workload Management
                    │
                    ├── View Employee Workload
                    ├── Identify Overloaded Employees
                    ├── Check Employee Availability
                    ├── Balance Task Distribution
                    └── Reassign Tasks When Required
    
                5. Attendance Management
                    │
                    ├── View Employee Attendance
                    ├── Review Late Records
                    ├── Review Absent Records
                    ├── Review Unknown / Off-site Locations
                    ├── View Leave / Late Requests
                    ├── Approve / Reject Leave
                    └── Adjust Workload When Required
    
                6. Team Communication
                    │
                    ├── Send Team Announcements
                    ├── Send Employee-Specific Messages
                    ├── Share Task Instructions
                    ├── Send Automated Deadline Reminders
                    └── Receive Employee Updates
    
                7. Feedback & Evaluation
                    │
                    ├── Receive Feedback from Employees
                    ├── Review Employee Work
                    ├── Provide Feedback
                    ├── Audit Evaluation
                    └── Monitor Improvement
    
                8. Issues Management
                    │
                    ├── Identify Issue
                    ├── Assess Issue Severity
                    ├── Manager Review
                    ├── Resolve Issue
                    │      OR
                    ├── Escalate to Higher Authority
                    ├── Audit Issue Details
                    └── Monitor Improvement
    
        EMPLOYEE Dashboard:
    
            1.  Work Management
                │
                ├── My Task Workspace
                ├── Task Dashboard / Queue
                ├── View Task Details
                ├── View Priority
                ├── Track Due Date
                ├── Start / Continue Task Execution
                ├── Update Task Progress
                ├── Record Work Activity
                ├── Upload Supporting Evidence / Files
                ├── Submit Work for Review
                ├── Receive Manager Feedback
                ├── Make Corrections
                ├── Resubmit Work
                └── Complete Task
    
                Additionally - Right under the hood of Work managment:
                    Task Execution
                          ↓
                        Issue?
                          ├── No → Continue Work
                          │
                          └── Yes
                               ↓
                           Raise Issue Ticket
                               ↓
                           Assign Issue Severity
                               ↓
                           Manager Intervention?
                               ├── Yes → Manager Resolves → Continue Work
                               └── No  → Escalation / Further Action
    
                Again, additionally the following is also configured:
                    Work Activity
                    ├── Progress Updates
                    ├── Work Notes
                    ├── Time / Effort
                    └── Supporting Evidence
    
            2.  Attendance & Availability
                    │
                    ├── Check-In
                    ├── Check-Out
                    ├── Attendance History
                    ├── Work Hours
                    └── Availability Status
                        ├── Available
                        ├── Busy
                        ├── In Meeting
                        ├── On Break
                        ├── In Training
                        ├── Do Not Disturb
                        ├── Working Remotely
                        ├── On Leave
                        ├── Absent
                        └── Offline
    
            3. Request & Self-Service
                │
                ├── Leave Requests
                ├── Permission Requests
                ├── Scheduled Requests
                ├── Request Status
                ├── Request Logs
                ├── Manager Approval History
                ├── Task Clarification / Guidance
                └── Resource Requests
    
            4. Notification & Communication
                │
                ├── New Task Notification
                ├── Overdue Task Notification
                ├── Reassigned Task Notification
                ├── Deadline Alerts
                ├── Manager Feedback
                ├── Announcements
                └── Escalation Alerts
    
            5. Performance & Activity
                │
                ├── Task Activity Summary
                ├── Completion Metrics
                ├── Attendance Summary
                ├── Activity History
                └── Feedback History
    
            6. Profile & Security
                │
                ├── My Profile
                ├── Account Details
                ├── Request Account Changes
                ├── Change Password
                └── Active Sessions
    
        --------------------------------------
        Core Manager-Employee Task Lifecycle
        --------------------------------------
    
        MANAGER
           ↓
        Create Task
           ↓
        Define Deliverables
           ↓
        Set Priority & Due Date
           ↓
        Check Employee Workload
           ↓
        Assign Employee
           ↓
        Notify Employee
           ↓
        EMPLOYEE
           ↓
        Start / Continue Work
           ↓
        Update Progress
           ↓
        Record Work Activity
           ↓
        Upload Evidence
           ↓
        Submit Work

    const C={navy:['#19344A','#19344A'],blue:['#EAF3F9','#32749A'],orange:['#FFF1E4','#C66B17'],green:['#E8F5EF','#2D7B5C'],purple:['#F0EBFA','#70529D'],red:['#FBEDED','#B35C65'],gray:['#F5F7FA','#6B7B8D']};

    function page(id,title,subtitle,h=1350){p={id,title,h,cells:[],nodes:[]};pages.push(p);box(title,40,25,1520,55,'navy','bar');box(subtitle,40,90,1520,45,'gray','note');if(id!=='overview')box('â† OVERVIEW',1350,145,210,36,'purple','link','overview');return p;}
    function box(label,x,y,w=240,h=74,color='blue',kind='box',link,parent='1'){
     const id=p.id+'-n'+(++seq),cc=C[color];let style='whiteSpace=wrap;html=1;fontFamily=Arial;fontSize=14;spacing=10;strokeWidth=1.3;shadow=0;';
     style+=(kind==='decision'?'rhombus;':kind==='data'?'shape=cylinder3;size=12;':kind==='note'?'rounded=0;strokeOpacity=0;align=left;':kind==='bar'?'rounded=1;fontStyle=1;fontSize=19;':'rounded=1;arcSize=12;');
     style+=`fillColor=${cc[0]};strokeColor=${cc[1]};fontColor=${color==='navy'?'#FFFFFF':'#1B2935'};`;
     if(kind==='link')style+='fontStyle=1;dashed=1;';
     const attrs=`label="${esc(label.replace(/\n/g,'<br>'))}"${link?` link="data:page/id,${link}"`:''}`;
     p.cells.push(`<object id="${id}" ${attrs}><mxCell style="${style}" vertex="1" parent="${parent}"><mxGeometry x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" as="geometry"/></mxCell></object>`);
     p.nodes.push({id,label,x,y,w,h,parent});return id;
    }
    function edge(a,b,label='',color='gray',style=''){const id=p.id+'-e'+(++seq);if(!style.includes('exitX')){const na=p.nodes.find(n=>n.id===a),nb=p.nodes.find(n=>n.id===b);if(nb.y>na.y+30)style+='exitX=0.5;exitY=1;entryX=0.5;entryY=0;';else if(nb.y<na.y-30)style+='exitX=0.5;exitY=0;entryX=0.5;entryY=1;';else if(nb.x>na.x)style+='exitX=1;exitY=0.5;entryX=0;entryY=0.5;';else style+='exitX=0;exitY=0.5;entryX=1;entryY=0.5;';}p.cells.push(`<mxCell id="${id}" value="${esc(label)}" style="edgeStyle=orthogonalEdgeStyle;rounded=1;html=1;endArrow=block;endFill=1;fontFamily=Arial;fontSize=12;labelBackgroundColor=#FFFFFF;strokeColor=${C[color][1]};strokeWidth=1.4;${style}" edge="1" parent="1" source="${a}" target="${b}"><mxGeometry relative="1" as="geometry"/></mxCell>`);}
    function chain(nodes,color='gray'){for(let i=1;i<nodes.length;i++)edge(nodes[i-1],nodes[i],'',color);return nodes;}
    function relabel(a,b,label){p.cells=p.cells.map(s=>s.includes(`source="${a}" target="${b}"`)?s.replace('value=""',`value="${esc(label)}"`):s);}
    function decisionNode(id,label){p.cells=p.cells.map(s=>s.includes(`<object id="${id}"`)?s.replace(/label="[^"]*"/,`label="${esc(label)}"`).replace('rounded=1;arcSize=12;','rhombus;'):s);p.nodes.find(n=>n.id===id).label=label;}
    function bar(text,y,color='navy'){return box(text,40,y,1520,40,color,'bar');}
    function flow(title,x,y,steps,color='blue',w=325,gap=112,links={}){box(title,x,y,w,46,color,'bar');return chain(steps.map((t,i)=>box(t,x+12,y+72+i*gap,w-24,78,color,links[i]?'link':'box',links[i])),color);}
    function note(t,y,h=55){return box(t,40,y,1520,h,'gray','note');}
    function go(t,x,y,id,color='purple',w=300){return box(t,x,y,w,60,color,'link',id);}

## Assistant

Layer 1 will be copied from the original, including its shapes, wording, and connections. Layer 2 will keep the three familiar Admin, Manager, and Employee sections, with all ten roles and their responsibilities inside them. The database will explicitly collect login records, work history, evidence, attendance, requests, decisions, and audit logs before feeding Layer 3.

    box('AVAILABILITY OPTIONS\nAvailable • Busy • In Meeting • On Break • In Training • Do Not Disturb • Working Remotely • On Leave • Absent • Offline\nAttendance, session activity and task effort remain separate records.',4600,4160,2000,115,'green');

    box('WORK ACTIVITY ≠ TASK STATE\nProgress updates, work notes, effort entries and files are historical activity records.     const database=box('SHARED OPERATIONAL RECORDS & AUDIT DATABASE\n\nA • Layer 1 login, session and location records    +    B • All Layer 2 role, work and service records\n\nCurrent records + complete historical changes + file / evidence references\nLinked CRM lead, member, brokerage and payment status with source / verification time\n\nREAD OUTPUT → authorised analytics, reports, employee history, manager drill-down and Chairman appraisal\nWRITE INPUT → new actions and decisions continue the operational history',1650,6470,3500,310,'purple','database');
